# REFACTOR Domain Rows — Batch ES-2026-05-08-REFACTOR

Compact DPO rows for the REFACTOR adapter (sub-phase 4.4 — Refactoring
and Code Quality). REFACTOR is the smallest domain by volume in the
calibration distribution (~2 %) but every row must teach: refactor =
internal improvement without behaviour change.

The most common confusion patterns:
- 4.4 (refactor) vs 4.1 (feature implementation) — refactor must NOT add behaviour
- 4.4 (refactor) vs 3.x (architecture) — refactor follows arch decision; doesn't make it
- 4.4 (refactor) vs BUG (4.1 fix) — refactor preserves behaviour; bug-fix changes behaviour
- 4.4 (refactor) vs PLAN — refactor is a single coherent change; plan is multi-task

## Common coverage labels (apply to every row in this file)

```
domain_label:         REFACTOR
source_license_state: external_license_checked
contract_signature:   DomainProcessorUnit + DomainTranslatorUnit
node_role:            row_level_pair
pair_kind:            flow_node
coverage_status:      covered
attribution_required: false
phase:                4
sub_phase:            4.4   (every row in this file)
task_type:            refactoring-code-quality
```

Format: `[ID | type/OR/trans] sources | A | B | P | R`

## §3 — REFACTOR vs BUG / vs FEATURE / vs ARCH cross-domain confusion

[REFACTOR-0151 | C/0.30/repair] cwe-89 | A:"Refactor the SQL builder to use parameterised queries" | B:BUG/4.1 fix SQL injection (CWE-89) | P:REFACTOR/4.4 cosmetic refactor | R:[WRONG: parameterising fixes a known SQLi defect — that's a BUG repair, not refactor; adversarially mislabeled as REFACTOR]
[REFACTOR-0152 | C/0.32/repair] cwe-79 | A:"Refactor the template engine to add HTML encoding" | B:BUG/4.2 fix XSS | P:REFACTOR/4.4 add-encoding-helper | R:[WRONG: encoding to mitigate XSS = BUG fix not refactor]

## §12 — More refactor archetypes (REFACTOR-0451 through REFACTOR-0650)

DPO note per v15: every refactor chosen B preserves behaviour; rejected
P adds new behaviour. Token overlap kept low because chosen names
preservation tokens (keep-shape, no-runtime-change) and rejected names
behaviour-change tokens (add-feature, change-policy).

behaviour-change tokens (add-feature, change-policy).
